## Open

### wp-admin menus (owner, 12 Nov 2026)
- **Live 12 Nov 2026 (f645c1e8, checked in the browser):** each Wugweb plugin has its own top-level menu, in one block after a separator: Kits, DirectoryWeb, SourceWeb, RankWeb, InsightWeb, MailWeb, PlanWeb. There is no Animal Writes menu: it is the site layer and goes with the child theme, so its screens stay in Kits. Kits has five items (Home, Theme Options, Settings, Tools, Help) and stays lit on Keys and Modules. Linked campaigns is listed once, under Classification. Social posts is under News; Press coverage is its own menu. Claims is under Fluent Forms, after Entries; the screen and its address are unchanged and it stays read only. Kits → Modules has fields for Emails from forms, Full newsletter form and Sample listings. **Also live 12 Nov (0e83b81, 06f936c, 64221c0; checked in the browser):** every option and meta value that only WP-CLI could change has a field: RankWeb Settings (listings kept indexed, default share image), SourceWeb Settings (backfill date), Kits → Modules (district helplines, issuer short names), Kits → Settings → Analytics (GA4 via GTM, read only), Category (card image, pinned Resources), Linked campaigns (short name), Library item (Dated), campaign (Metrics). **Next (Phase 2):** tab grouping in InsightWeb and SourceWeb; Settings API, `wp_admin_notice` and `WP_List_Table` conversions; duplicate helpers. Inventory: `docs/admin-ia-inventory.md`.

### Martech audit fixes and SEO work (owner, 12 Nov 2026). Order 1, 2, 4, 3, 8, 9, 5, 6, 10–14
- **Hub lists with an array filter (item 1):** `/news/?state[]=kerala`, and on News, Resources, Library and Directory any `?x[]=…` whose list had more than one page, answered 500. Cause: the pagination links, the old `?query-N-page` redirect, the Directory sort link and the Directory place redirect ran `array_map( 'rawurlencode' )` over the query (a TypeError in PHP 8 on a list value). Fixed with `map_deep` (353f5899). Nothing was in the PHP log: the site writes no fatal errors there.
- **Opportunities sitemap (item 2):** `aw_opportunity-sitemap.xml` was in `sitemap_index.xml` and answered 404 (all 16 opportunities closed). The type is left out through `rank_math/sitemap/exclude_post_type` while none is open (73a03cf8). `aw_listing_type-sitemap.xml` is no longer in the index and no such taxonomy exists in the code: the 16 hits are crawlers' old copies; it goes on the 410 list of item 5.
- **"/https:/animalwrites.in" (item 4):** not in templates, patterns, menus, posts, post meta, options, mail tables or the live HTML of 10 pages; the 404 Monitor has no referer for it. Such paths (and `/animalwrites.in/…`) now 301 to the page they meant (3091ffee).
- **Interactivity import 404 (item 3):** every page prints the import map in its head before the modules, so browsers never ask for `…/navigation/@wordpress/interactivity`; crawlers that ignore import maps do (core's navigation `view.min.js`, 61 hits; the theme's `dropdown.js`, 4). Those exact paths 301 to core's interactivity module (733f0f50). kits-dropdown's registration is right.
- **Live check of items 1–4 (12 Nov, after 27653180 shipped):** News, Resources and Library with `state[]`, `tag[]`, `publisher[]`, `foo[]` answer 200, `follow, noindex`; the index lists no Opportunities sitemap; both redirects answer 301. `/directory/?state[]=kerala` still answered 500 (a second cause: the filter-form redirect ran `sanitize_title()` on the raw value), fixed in 32282b8c.
- **Hub filter dropdowns did not open:** the `kits-dropdown` module queued while the band renders was never printed (registered on live, checked with `wp eval`). Hub pages queue it on `wp_enqueue_scripts` (8a9adff6). **Checked on live 12 Nov:** the Publisher dropdown on /news/ opens (aria-expanded false → true); the module is printed on News, Resources, Library and Directory.
- **Stale asset paths (item 5):** kits-core `ad-slot.js` and Core `visit.php` are in no file of the build; none of 20 live pages prints them or a `plugins/home/u544169507/…/aw_deploy/…` path (all 20 use the current kits-cache file). Those exact paths and `aw_listing_type`, `aw_guide`, `news` sitemaps answer 410 (487c2b23); `news-sitemap.xml` itself is the Google News sitemap from the indexing PR on (numbered copies stay 410). `aw_topic-sitemap.xml` keeps its 301. Old kits-cache CSS stays 404: the names come from each build.
- **Speed (item 6): aw-analytics.js minified, live 12 Nov (owner's yes; 55a36e24).** Served as `aw-analytics.min.js`: 21,181 → 12,451 bytes, 6,622 → 4,140 sent (brotli). `tools/ci/js_min.py` writes it with a pinned terser; the Deploy check fails when it is not the source minified; SCRIPT_DEBUG loads the source. - **Mailweb: campaign emails fell back to PHP mail() (found 3 Nov, cause found, fixed 3 Nov).** Cause: each deploy re-clones aw_deploy, and the plugins in wp-content/plugins are links into it, so for a few seconds mailweb is not loaded. The cb905419 deploy (13:23 UTC) landed while campaign 2 was sending: from 13:23:11 to 13:23:21 FluentCRM sent 44 emails through PHP mail(), with no queue rows and no 1-a-second limit. 33 failed and 11 (contacts 22–31, 68) were marked sent but almost certainly never left. Fixes: (1) `tools/aw-ship.sh` refuses to deploy while a FluentCRM campaign is sending (pause it, ship, resume); (1b) a push straight to wordpress-dev deploys through the Deploy check job, which skipped (1), so the job now has the same check: it reads the site's `/wp-json/aw/v1/deploy-status` (yes or no only; `tools/ci/mail_sending_check.py`, used by aw-ship.sh too) and does not move `wordpress` while a campaign is sending or the site can't say; a manual run with "skip_mail_check" overrides it in an emergency; (2) AW Mail Guard (`tools/server/aw-mail-guard/`), installed once from Plugins → Add New → Upload so it sits outside aw_deploy: while mailweb is missing it holds FluentCRM batches and sends any other email through the same SMTP mailbox, never PHP mail(); (3) the 11 get the email again as a copy of campaign 2 (tag "Lab rules newsletter resend 2, 4 Nov"). Rows left "sending" are retried by mailweb after 10 minutes. **Owner:** install the AW Mail Guard zip.

- **CDN header and the aw_fv cookie (owner: "Yes, fix it", 3 Nov 2026, live in f135d28a):** the visit cookie aw_fv (aw-analytics.js) no longer keeps a page out of the shared cache (cdn-cache.php); other aw_ and kits_ cookies still do. Before, a returning visitor who loaded a page first after a purge left LiteSpeed holding a copy with max-age=0, so the CDN skipped it (seen on Lab after deploys). Checked on live: Lab s-maxage=900 with and without the cookie, CDN HIT. Shipped with the Deploy check's skip_mail_check (owner: "Yes, override"), because FluentCRM campaigns 3 and 4 hold 11 scheduled emails each that stopped moving at 22:47 IST on 3 Nov; while they wait, the mail gate refuses every deploy.
